=== FILE: src/nd_import_diag.h ===
// STAGE B(neverdead-imports): implementacion REAL de los 68 stubs de import
// inline del XEX (0x827BD8A4..0x827BE5D4) que el codegen dejo como basura.
//
// Contexto: la tabla XEX2 de import libraries apunta a otra region que el
// juego no llama; las llamadas reales van a estos thunks de 16 bytes. El
// runtime (rexruntime.dll/lib) trae la tabla ordinal->nombre de xboxkrnl.exe
// (verificado en runtime: NDIDUMP, log neverdead_029) y los wrappers
// __imp__<Nombre>(ctx, base) como simbolos (llvm-nm: presentes; mismo
// mecanismo que usa el codegen de ReXGlue en NFSMW). Aqui cada stub se
// intercepta con REX_HOOK_RAW (simbolo fuerte que reemplaza el alias debil
// del recompilado) y se delega al wrapper real.
//
// Los que NO tienen wrapper en el lib quedan como NdiDiag (no-op + log):
// se trataran si el boot los toca.
//
// sub_827BD8F4 (0x155 KeTlsSetValue) se implementa en nd_ke_tls_fix.h.

#pragma once

#include <chrono>
#include <condition_variable>
#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
#include <mutex>
#include <shared_mutex>
#include <thread>
#include <unordered_map>

#ifdef _WIN32
#ifndef NOMINMAX
#define NOMINMAX
#endif
#ifndef WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#define WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#endif
#include <windows.h>
#endif

#include <rex/hook.h>
#include <rex/logging.h>
#include <rex/runtime.h>
#include <rex/system/export_resolver.h>

//=============================================================================
// M2/W3 -- conmutadores del CRT NATIVO de ReXGlue (tabla [rexcrt])
//=============================================================================
// El mecanismo nativo de ReXGlue ([rexcrt] en config/crt.toml) mapea nombres
// de funcion del CRT a direcciones del invitado, las renombra a
// `rexcrt_<Nombre>`, las EXCLUYE de la recompilacion y deja que las resuelva
// la implementacion nativa de rexruntime.dll.
//
// Estos conmutadores existen para RETIRAR los hooks caseros cuando [rexcrt]
// cubre de verdad esa funcion, y poder volver atras sin editar codigo:
//   NEVERDEAD_NATIVE_CRT_MEMCPY = 1 -> el memcpy casero (nd_indirect_trap.h)
//                                    se retira y manda rexcrt_memcpy.
//   NEVERDEAD_NATIVE_CRT_HEAP   = 1 -> los bump-allocators caseros se retiran
//                                    y manda el grupo heap nativo.
//
// DEFECTO: 0 (= comportamiento actual, identico byte a byte al de hoy).
//
// ADVERTENCIA (ver .agents/teamwork/worker/handoff_w3.md):
//   * El grupo heap ([rexcrt] heap) NO esta declarado todavia: falta una
//     RtlSizeHeap probada. NEVERDEAD_NATIVE_CRT_HEAP DEBE quedarse en 0.
//     Con el grupo heap declarado pero el GetDefaultHeap casero retirado, el
//     CRT invitado aborta con R6030 ("CRT not initialized").
//   * NEVERDEAD_NATIVE_CRT_MEMCPY solo es seguro cuando apply_bink_branch_fix.py
//     es no-op (arbol v2, donde memcpy es una sola funcion): los 3 call sites
//     que usan neverdead_memcpy_8278AEEC apuntan a la ETIQUETA INTERIOR
//     0x8278AEEC (cola desalineada del memcpy), no a la entrada 0x8278AE60, y
//     la copia nativa es una memcpy COMPLETA (la cola nativa copia solo el
//     resto). Sustituir la cola por la memcpy completa copiaria de mas.
#ifndef NEVERDEAD_NATIVE_CRT_MEMCPY
#define NEVERDEAD_NATIVE_CRT_MEMCPY 0
#endif
#ifndef NEVERDEAD_NATIVE_CRT_HEAP
#define NEVERDEAD_NATIVE_CRT_HEAP 0
#endif

namespace neverdead::sync {

struct SafeLock {
  std::mutex internal_mtx;
  std::condition_variable cv;
  uint32_t owner_tid{0};
  int32_t depth{0};

  void Enter() {
    const uint32_t tid = static_cast<uint32_t>(GetCurrentThreadId());
    std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(internal_mtx);
    while (owner_tid != 0 && owner_tid != tid) {
      cv.wait(lock);
    }
    owner_tid = tid;
    ++depth;
  }

  bool TryEnter() {
    const uint32_t tid = static_cast<uint32_t>(GetCurrentThreadId());
    std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(internal_mtx);
    if (owner_tid == 0 || owner_tid == tid) {
      owner_tid = tid;
      ++depth;
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  void Leave() {
    const uint32_t tid = static_cast<uint32_t>(GetCurrentThreadId());
    std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(internal_mtx);
    if (owner_tid != tid || depth <= 0) {
      // Unbalanced leave or not owner: ignore defensively to prevent deadlock/hang
      return;
    }
    --depth;
    if (depth == 0) {
      owner_tid = 0;
      cv.notify_one();
    }
  }
};

class LockManager {
 public:
  static LockManager& Instance() {
    static LockManager inst;
    return inst;
  }

  SafeLock* GetOrCreate(uint32_t cs) {
    {
      std::shared_lock<std::shared_mutex> read_lock(table_mtx_);
      auto it = locks_.find(cs);
      if (it != locks_.end()) {
        return it->second.get();
      }
    }
    std::unique_lock<std::shared_mutex> write_lock(table_mtx_);
    auto& ptr = locks_[cs];
    if (!ptr) {
      ptr = std::make_unique<SafeLock>();
    }
    return ptr.get();
  }

 private:
  std::shared_mutex table_mtx_;
  std::unordered_map<uint32_t, std::unique_ptr<SafeLock>> locks_;
};

}  // namespace neverdead::sync

namespace neverdead::fix {

inline const char* NdiResolve(rex::runtime::ExportResolver* res,
                              const char* module, uint16_t ordinal,
                              bool* implemented) {
  if (res == nullptr) {
    return nullptr;
  }
  auto* e = res->GetExportByOrdinal(module, ordinal);
  if (e == nullptr) {
    return nullptr;
  }
  if (implemented != nullptr) {
    *implemented = e->is_implemented();
  }
  return e->name;
}

inline void NdiDiag(uint32_t va, uint16_t ordinal, bool* once) {
  if (*once) {
    return;
  }
  *once = true;
  rex::Runtime* rt = rex::Runtime::instance();
  rex::runtime::ExportResolver* res =
      (rt != nullptr) ? rt->export_resolver() : nullptr;
  bool kimpl = false;
  bool ximpl = false;
  const char* kname = NdiResolve(res, "xboxkrnl.exe", ordinal, &kimpl);
  const char* xname = NdiResolve(res, "xam.xex", ordinal, &ximpl);
  REXLOG_INFO(
      "neverdead: IMPORT-SIN-WRAPPER va=0x{:08X} ord=0x{:03X} | "
      "xboxkrnl='{}'(impl={}) | xam='{}'(impl={})",
      va, ordinal, kname ? kname : "-", kimpl, xname ? xname : "-", ximpl);
}

}  // namespace neverdead::fix

// STAGE B: llamada real al wrapper del runtime (probados en rexruntime.lib).
#define ND_IMPORT_REAL(sym, va, ord, fname)                              \
  REX_EXTERN(__imp__##fname);                                            \
  REX_HOOK_RAW(sym) {                                                    \
    static bool nd_called = false;                                       \
    if (!nd_called) {                                                    \
      nd_called = true;                                                  \
      REXLOG_INFO(                                                       \
          "neverdead: IMPORT-REAL va=0x{:08X} ord=0x{:03X} {} lr=0x{:08X}", \
          va, ord, #fname, (uint32_t)ctx.lr);                            \
    }                                                                    \
    __imp__##fname(ctx, base);                                           \
  }

// Pendiente: sin wrapper en lib -> no-op + log (identificado por NdiDiag).
#define ND_IMPORT_DIAG(sym, va, ord)                  \
  REX_HOOK_RAW(sym) {                                 \
    static bool nd_once = false;                      \
    neverdead::fix::NdiDiag(va, ord, &nd_once);       \
    (void)ctx;                                        \
    (void)base;                                       \
  }

// Traza completa del ciclo de vida CS (init/enter/leave con args y lr).

inline bool NdiIsInvalidOrDummyCS(uint32_t cs) {
  return (cs < 0x10000u || (cs & 0xFFFF0000u) == 0x3F800000u || cs == 0x3D634BD7u);
}

REX_HOOK_RAW(sub_827BD8A4) {  // 0x12E RtlInitializeCriticalSection
  const uint32_t cs = ctx.r3.u32;
  if (NdiIsInvalidOrDummyCS(cs)) {
    return;
  }
  neverdead::sync::LockManager::Instance().GetOrCreate(cs);
}

REX_HOOK_RAW(sub_827BD8B4) {  // 0x125 RtlEnterCriticalSection
  const uint32_t cs = ctx.r3.u32;
  if (NdiIsInvalidOrDummyCS(cs)) {
    return;
  }
  auto* lock = neverdead::sync::LockManager::Instance().GetOrCreate(cs);
  lock->Enter();
}

REX_HOOK_RAW(sub_827BD8C4) {  // 0x130 RtlLeaveCriticalSection
  const uint32_t cs = ctx.r3.u32;
  if (NdiIsInvalidOrDummyCS(cs)) {
    return;
  }
  auto* lock = neverdead::sync::LockManager::Instance().GetOrCreate(cs);
  lock->Leave();
}

REX_HOOK_RAW(sub_827BD904) {  // 0x12F RtlInitializeCriticalSectionAndSpinCount
  const uint32_t cs = ctx.r3.u32;
  if (NdiIsInvalidOrDummyCS(cs)) {
    ctx.r3.u64 = 1;
    return;
  }
  neverdead::sync::LockManager::Instance().GetOrCreate(cs);
  ctx.r3.u64 = 1;
}
ND_IMPORT_REAL(sub_827BD914, 0x827BD914u, 0x152, KeTlsAlloc)
REX_HOOK_RAW(sub_827BD924) {  // 0x141 RtlTryEnterCriticalSection
  const uint32_t cs = ctx.r3.u32;
  if (NdiIsInvalidOrDummyCS(cs)) {
    ctx.r3.u64 = 1;
    return;
  }
  auto* lock = neverdead::sync::LockManager::Instance().GetOrCreate(cs);
  bool ok = lock->TryEnter();
  ctx.r3.u64 = ok ? 1 : 0;
}

// FIX(neverdead-mm-get-phys): Hook real xboxkrnl#0x0BE (MmGetPhysicalAddress)
// Translates guest virtual address in r3 to physical address for VdInitializeRingBuffer.
// If rex export resolver returns 0 on untracked guest heap, fallback to Xbox 360 direct map (& 0x1FFFFFFF).
REX_HOOK_RAW(sub_827BDF64) {
  static bool s_diag_printed = false;
  const uint32_t virt_in = ctx.r3.u32;
  __imp__MmGetPhysicalAddress(ctx, base);
  if (ctx.r3.u32 == 0 && virt_in != 0) {
    ctx.r3.u64 = virt_in & 0x1FFFFFFFu;
    REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: MmGetPhysicalAddress virt=0x{:08X} -> fallback phys=0x{:08X} lr=0x{:08X}",
                virt_in, ctx.r3.u32, (uint32_t)ctx.lr);
  } else if (!s_diag_printed) {
    s_diag_printed = true;
    REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: MmGetPhysicalAddress virt=0x{:08X} -> phys=0x{:08X} lr=0x{:08X}",
                virt_in, ctx.r3.u32, (uint32_t)ctx.lr);
  }
}

              phys_base, log2_size, size_bytes, (uint32_t)ctx.lr);

// FIX(neverdead-rtlmultibytetounicoden): Safe RtlMultiByteToUnicodeN hook
REX_HOOK_RAW(sub_827BE5D4) {
  uint32_t dst = ctx.r3.u32;
  uint32_t max_bytes = ctx.r4.u32;
  uint32_t p_bytes = ctx.r5.u32;
  uint32_t src = ctx.r6.u32;
  uint32_t src_bytes = ctx.r7.u32;

  REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: RtlMultiByteToUnicodeN dst=0x{:08X} max_b={} p_b=0x{:08X} src=0x{:08X} src_b={} lr=0x{:08X}",
              dst, max_bytes, p_bytes, src, src_bytes, (uint32_t)ctx.lr);

  if (dst == 0 || max_bytes == 0 || src == 0) {
    if (p_bytes != 0) REX_STORE_U32(p_bytes, 0);
    ctx.r3.u64 = 0;
    return;
  }

  uint32_t chars_to_copy = max_bytes / 2;
  if (src_bytes > 0 && src_bytes < chars_to_copy) {
    chars_to_copy = src_bytes;
  }
  uint32_t written = 0;
  for (uint32_t i = 0; i < chars_to_copy; ++i) {
    uint8_t ch = REX_LOAD_U8(src + i);
    REX_STORE_U16(dst + i * 2, static_cast<uint16_t>(ch));
    written += 2;
    if (ch == 0 && src_bytes == 0) break;
  }
  if (p_bytes != 0) {
    REX_STORE_U32(p_bytes, written);
  }
  ctx.r3.u64 = 0;
}

// FIX(neverdead-sub_827BDF34): Ordinal 0x052 is RtlSetLastWin32Error / SetLastError.
// Mismapped previously to ExGetXConfigSetting, which clobbered ctx.r3 with 0xC000000D
// and flooded logs with 866k Unimplemented XConfig category 0x001E messages.
REX_HOOK_RAW(sub_827BDF34) {
  ::SetLastError(ctx.r3.u32);
}

// FIX(neverdead-ptd-alloc): _getptd_noexit (sub_8278BCA0) resilient thread-local allocation.
// Evita el bump allocator descontrolado (64KB por llamada, miles de llamadas)
// asignando un único bloque de 4KB por hilo para preservar _tiddata y errno.
REX_HOOK_RAW(sub_8278BCA0) {
  static thread_local uint32_t s_thread_ptd = 0;
  if (s_thread_ptd == 0) {
    static std::atomic<uint32_t> s_ptd_alloc{0x30100000u};
    uint32_t ptd = s_ptd_alloc.fetch_add(0x1000u);
    void* host_ptr = reinterpret_cast<void*>(reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(base) + ptd);
    VirtualAlloc(host_ptr, 0x1000, MEM_COMMIT, PAGE_READWRITE);
    std::memset(host_ptr, 0, 0x1000);
    s_thread_ptd = ptd;
    REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: _getptd (sub_8278BCA0) initialized PTD for thread at guest=0x{:08X}", ptd);
  }
  ctx.r3.u64 = s_thread_ptd;
  ctx.r31.u64 = s_thread_ptd;
}

=== FILE: src/nd_indirect_guard.h ===
// DIAG(neverdead-indirect-guard): sonda de diagnostico para saltos indirectos.
//
// Solo LOGUEA cuando el target cae fuera del rango de codigo recompilado
// [0x821A0000, 0x827C05E4). NO cambia la semantica valida: tras loguear, el
// dispatch original (REX_CALL_INDIRECT_FUNC / ResolveIndirectFunction) sigue
// su curso sin alteraciones: sin returns, sin throws, sin mutar ctx/base.
//
// Uso en codigo generado (via apply_indirect_guard.py, nunca a mano):
//   ctx.lr = 0x824D9EAC;
//   ND_DIAG_INDIRECT(ctx, base, nd_r11_pre_9E94, 0x824D9EAC);
//   REX_CALL_INDIRECT_FUNC(ctx.ctr.u32);
//
// El header es dual-use: compila tanto en TUs recomp (pch con logging) como
// en el host (neverdead_app.h). Solo depende de <rex/logging.h>.
// FASE4-HOST: verificado que `base` (uint8_t* arena) esta en scope en todo
// DEFINE_REX_FUNC (firma ctx,base) y que REXLOG_ERROR existe con formato fmt.

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>

#include <rex/logging.h>

namespace neverdead::diag {

// Rango de codigo recompilado (tabla de funcs del log 010). Coincide con
// REX_CODE_BASE / REX_CODE_SIZE del pch (0x821A0000 + 0x6205E4 = 0x827C05E4).
inline constexpr uint32_t kCodeBase = 0x821A0000u;
inline constexpr uint32_t kCodeEnd = 0x827C05E4u;  // exclusivo

// Ventana de imagen guest para el peek seguro (REX_IMAGE_BASE/SIZE del pch:
// 0x82000000 + 0xC10000 = 0x82C10000, coincide con el log 010).
inline constexpr uint32_t kImageBase = 0x82000000u;
inline constexpr uint32_t kImageSize = 0x00C10000u;

inline constexpr bool InCodeRange(uint32_t addr) {
  return addr >= kCodeBase && addr < kCodeEnd;
}

// Lectura big-endian segura (memcpy: sin problemas de alineamiento ni
// aliasing). Solo llamar con guest dentro de [kImageBase, kImageBase+size).
inline uint32_t PeekGuestBe32(const uint8_t* base, uint32_t guest) {
  uint32_t raw = 0;
  std::memcpy(&raw, base + guest, sizeof(raw));
  return (raw >> 24) | ((raw >> 8) & 0xFF00u) | ((raw << 8) & 0xFF0000u) |
         (raw << 24);
}

}  // namespace neverdead::diag

// ND_DIAG_INDIRECT(ctx, base, r11_pre, lr_site): loguea r11_pre/lr/target solo
// si el target (ctx.ctr, ya fijado por el mtctr del guest) esta fuera de rango.
// El peek de guest[target] solo se intenta dentro de la ventana de imagen para
// no leer fuera de la arena (p. ej. target 0xFFFFFBFF + offset fisico saldria
// de la arena de 4GB). Sin efectos secundarios: no toca ctx ni base.
#define ND_DIAG_INDIRECT(ctx, base, r11_pre, lr_site)                        \
  do {                                                                       \
    const uint32_t nd_target_ = static_cast<uint32_t>((ctx).ctr.u32);        \
    if (nd_target_ < ::neverdead::diag::kCodeBase ||                         \
        nd_target_ >= ::neverdead::diag::kCodeEnd) {                         \
      uint32_t nd_peek_ = 0;                                                 \
      const char* nd_peek_st_ = "n/a";                                       \
      if (nd_target_ >= ::neverdead::diag::kImageBase &&                     \
          nd_target_ <=                                                      \
              ::neverdead::diag::kImageBase + ::neverdead::diag::kImageSize - \
                  4u) {                                                      \
        nd_peek_ = ::neverdead::diag::PeekGuestBe32(                         \
            reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(base), nd_target_);             \
        nd_peek_st_ = "ok";                                                  \
      }                                                                      \
      REXLOG_ERROR(                                                          \
          "neverdead: indirect fuera de rango r11_pre=0x{:08X} lr=0x{:08X} "  \
          "target=0x{:08X} peek={} (0x{:08X})",                              \
          static_cast<uint32_t>(r11_pre), static_cast<uint32_t>(lr_site),    \
          nd_target_, nd_peek_st_, nd_peek_);                                \
    }                                                                        \
  } while (0)


=== FILE: src/nd_indirect_trap.h ===
// DIAG-TEMPORAL(neverdead-indirect-trap): override del dispatch indirecto.
//
// Solo para cazar el caller de saltos OOB. Loguea el lr y target cuando el
// target cae fuera de [0x821A0000, 0x827C05E4) y evita el abort fatal ignorando
// llamadas a memoria no mapeada/no codigo.

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>

#include <rex/logging.h>

//=============================================================================
// M2/W3 -- conmutador del memcpy NATIVO de ReXGlue ([rexcrt])
//=============================================================================
// NEVERDEAD_NATIVE_CRT_MEMCPY = 1 retira la implementacion casera de la cola
// de memcpy (neverdead_memcpy_8278AEEC).  DEFECTO: 0 (= comportamiento actual).
//
// POR QUE HAY QUE DEJARLA (evidencia con Capstone, ver handoff_w3.md seccion 1):
//   0x8278AE60  = entrada de la funcion memcpy del CRT (dst=r3, src=r4, n=r5).
//   0x8278AEEC  = ETIQUETA INTERIOR de esa misma funcion: es la rama
//                 "n <= 0x80 y destino desalineado" (dcbtst / addi r4,-8 /
//                 addi r3,-8 y luego copia r7=(n>>3)&0xF qwords + r6=n&7 bytes).
//                 El llamante ya consumio la cabecera, por eso r5 va reducido.
//   Los 3 call sites que usan neverdead_memcpy_8278AEEC (0x8278B0A4,
//   0x8278B08C, 0x8278AFB4, parcheados por apply_bink_branch_fix.py) apuntan a
//   ESA ETIQUETA INTERIOR, no a la entrada 0x8278AE60.  La implementacion
//   nativa rexcrt_memcpy del runtime es una memcpy COMPLETA: sustituir la cola
//   por ella copiaria la cabecera dos veces.  Por tanto la cola casera NO es
//   redundante con [rexcrt] memcpy: NO se retira todavia.
//   Se puede poner a 1 SOLO cuando el arbol v2 haga que memcpy sea una sola
//   funcion (seed correcto) y apply_bink_branch_fix.py pase a ser no-op; entonce
//   los 3 call sites desaparecen y el simbolo deja de necesitarse (si queda
//   alguno, el enlazado falla con "símbolo no definido": es deliberado, para
//   que no se pueda activar el flag con el parche bink todavia activo).
#ifndef NEVERDEAD_NATIVE_CRT_MEMCPY
#define NEVERDEAD_NATIVE_CRT_MEMCPY 0
#endif

DECLARE_REX_FUNC(sub_8278EB50);
DECLARE_REX_FUNC(sub_82252E20);
DECLARE_REX_FUNC(sub_8278BD9C);
void sub_821E0FA0(PPCContext& ctx, uint8_t* base);

inline void neverdead_handler_824F6DB8(PPCContext& ctx, uint8_t* base) {
  ctx.r3.s64 = ctx.r1.s64 + 0x90;
  ctx.lr = 0x824F6DC0;
  sub_82252E20(ctx, base);
  ctx.r1.s64 += 0x100;
  sub_8278BD9C(ctx, base);
}

inline void neverdead_epilogue_8269987C(PPCContext& ctx, uint8_t* base) {
  uint32_t r11_val = (ctx.r16.u32 & 0xFF);
  bool flag = false;
  if (r11_val != 0 && (ctx.r18.u32 & 0xFF) != 0) {
    flag = true;
  } else {
    uint32_t w11 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r17.u32 - 17012);
    flag = (w11 != 254);
  }
  REX_STORE_U8((uint32_t)(-2102198272 + 18906), flag ? 1 : 0);
  uint32_t b1 = REX_LOAD_U8(ctx.r22.u32 + 1);
  if (b1 != 0 && REX_LOAD_U8(ctx.r22.u32 + 0) == 0) {
    ctx.r3.s64 = 0;
  } else {
    ctx.r3.u64 = ctx.r18.u64;
  }
  ctx.r1.s64 += 224;
  ctx.r16.u64 = REX_LOAD_U64(ctx.r1.u32 - 136);
  ctx.r17.u64 = REX_LOAD_U64(ctx.r1.u32 - 128);
  ctx.r18.u64 = REX_LOAD_U64(ctx.r1.u32 - 120);
  ctx.r19.u64 = REX_LOAD_U64(ctx.r1.u32 - 112);
  ctx.r20.u64 = REX_LOAD_U64(ctx.r1.u32 - 104);
  ctx.r21.u64 = REX_LOAD_U64(ctx.r1.u32 - 96);
  ctx.r22.u64 = REX_LOAD_U64(ctx.r1.u32 - 88);
  ctx.r23.u64 = REX_LOAD_U64(ctx.r1.u32 - 80);
  ctx.r24.u64 = REX_LOAD_U64(ctx.r1.u32 - 72);
  ctx.r25.u64 = REX_LOAD_U64(ctx.r1.u32 - 64);
  ctx.r26.u64 = REX_LOAD_U64(ctx.r1.u32 - 56);
  ctx.r27.u64 = REX_LOAD_U64(ctx.r1.u32 - 48);
  ctx.r28.u64 = REX_LOAD_U64(ctx.r1.u32 - 40);
  ctx.r29.u64 = REX_LOAD_U64(ctx.r1.u32 - 32);
  ctx.r30.u64 = REX_LOAD_U64(ctx.r1.u32 - 24);
  ctx.r31.u64 = REX_LOAD_U64(ctx.r1.u32 - 16);
  ctx.lr = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r1.u32 - 8);
}

#if !NEVERDEAD_NATIVE_CRT_MEMCPY
void neverdead_memcpy_8278AEEC(PPCContext& ctx, uint8_t* base);
#endif
void neverdead_epilogue_822CACC0(PPCContext& ctx, uint8_t* base);

#undef REX_CALL_INDIRECT_FUNC
#define REX_CALL_INDIRECT_FUNC(x)                                                \
  do {                                                                           \
    REX_PROFILE_INDIRECT_DISPATCH();                                             \
    uint32_t rex_trap_target_ = (uint32_t)(x);                                   \
    const uint32_t rex_r3_pre_ = ctx.r3.u32;                                     \
    if (rex_trap_target_ < 0x821A0000u ||                                         \
        rex_trap_target_ >= 0x827C05E4u) {                                        \
      ctx.r3.u64 = 0;                                                          \
      if (ctx.lr == 0x8242D02Cu) {                                               \
        ctx.r30.u64 = ctx.r29.u64;                                             \
      }                                                                         \
      /* DIAG(chunk-iterator): 0x821E0FBC es el bctrl del bucle del iterador de \
         chunks (sub_821E0FA0). Con target 0 significa que [r3] no es una vtable \
         valida o que [vtable+4] es 0. Se vuelca el estado UNA vez por ventana. */\
      if ((uint32_t)ctx.lr == 0x821E0FBCu) {                                     \
        static uint32_t s_ci_probe_ = 0;                                         \
        if (++s_ci_probe_ <= 3) {                                                \
          const uint32_t r3_ = rex_r3_pre_;                                      \
          const uint32_t r27_ = ctx.r27.u32;                                     \
          const uint32_t r28_ = ctx.r28.u32;                                     \
          const bool r27_ok_ = (r27_ >= 0x10000u && r27_ < 0x90000000u);          \
          const uint32_t off_ = r27_ok_ ? REX_LOAD_U32(r27_ + 20) : 0u;           \
          const uint32_t vt_ = (r3_ >= 0x10000u && r3_ < 0x80000000u)            \
                                   ? REX_LOAD_U32(r3_) : 0u;                     \
          const uint32_t fn_ = (vt_ >= 0x82000000u && vt_ < 0x827C05E4u)         \
                                   ? REX_LOAD_U32(vt_ + 4) : 0u;                \
          REXLOG_ERROR("neverdead: CI-PROBE #{} r3=0x{:08X} vt=0x{:08X} fn=0x{:08X} " \
                       "r24=0x{:08X} r25=0x{:08X} r26=0x{:08X} r27=0x{:08X} "     \
                       "r28=0x{:08X} r29=0x{:08X} [r27+20]=0x{:08X} target=0x{:08X}", \
                       s_ci_probe_, r3_, vt_, fn_, ctx.r24.u32, ctx.r25.u32,        \
                       ctx.r26.u32, r27_, r28_, ctx.r29.u32, off_, rex_trap_target_); \
        }                                                                        \
      }                                                                          \
      static uint32_t rex_oob_count_ = 0;                                        \
      if (++rex_oob_count_ <= 25 || (rex_oob_count_ % 100000 == 0)) {               \
        REXLOG_ERROR("neverdead: TRAP-OOB caller lr=0x{:08X} target=0x{:08X} (skipped, #{})", \
                     (uint32_t)ctx.lr, rex_trap_target_, rex_oob_count_);        \
      }                                                                           \
      break;                                                                     \
    }                                                                            \
    PPCFunc* rex_indirect_func_;                                                  \
    if ((uint32_t)(rex_trap_target_ - REX_CODE_BASE) <                            \
        REX_CODE_SIZE + REX_THUNK_RESERVE_SIZE) [[likely]] {                     \
      rex_indirect_func_ = REX_LOOKUP_FUNC(base, rex_trap_target_);              \
    } else {                                                                     \
      rex_indirect_func_ = nullptr;                                               \
    }                                                                            \
    if (!rex_indirect_func_) [[unlikely]] {                                      \
      ctx.last_indirect_target = rex_trap_target_;                               \
      if (rex_trap_target_ == 0x82378300u) {                                     \
        float f_ = (float)ctx.f0.f64;                                            \
        uint32_t u_;                                                             \
        std::memcpy(&u_, &f_, 4);                                                \
        REX_STORE_U32(ctx.r5.u32, u_);                                           \
        break;                                                                   \
      }                                                                          \
      if (rex_trap_target_ == 0x8269987Cu) {                                     \
        neverdead_epilogue_8269987C(ctx, base);                                  \
        break;                                                                   \
      }                                                                          \
      if (rex_trap_target_ == 0x822CACC0u) {                                     \
        neverdead_epilogue_822CACC0(ctx, base);                                  \
        break;                                                                   \
      }                                                                          \
      if (rex_trap_target_ == 0x824DDF90u) {                                     \
        REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: unregistered indirect call 0x824DDF90 lr=0x{:08X} (handled r3=1)", (uint32_t)ctx.lr); \
        ctx.r3.u64 = 1;                                                          \
        break;                                                                   \
      }                                                                          \
      if (rex_trap_target_ == 0x824F6DB8u) {                                     \
        REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: unregistered indirect call 0x824F6DB8 lr=0x{:08X} (stack-safe epilogue)", (uint32_t)ctx.lr); \
        neverdead_handler_824F6DB8(ctx, base);                                   \
        break;                                                                   \
      }                                                                          \
      REXLOG_WARN("neverdead: unregistered indirect call target=0x{:08X} lr=0x{:08X} (safe stub r3=0)", \
                  rex_trap_target_, (uint32_t)ctx.lr);                           \
      ctx.r3.u64 = 0;                                                            \
      break;                                                                     \
    }                                                                            \
    if (rex_indirect_func_) {                                                    \
      rex_indirect_func_(ctx, base);                                             \
    }                                                                            \
  } while (0)

inline void neverdead_epilogue_822CACC0(PPCContext& ctx, uint8_t* base) {
  const uint32_t r30_val = (ctx.r30.u32 >= 0x82000000u) ? ctx.r30.u32 : 0x828E0000u;
  const uint32_t obj = (r30_val >= 0x10000u) ? REX_LOAD_U32(r30_val + 4876) : 0;
  static bool s_epilogue_logged = false;
  if (!s_epilogue_logged) {
    s_epilogue_logged = true;
    REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: epilogue_822CACC0 enter r1=0x{:08X} r30=0x{:08X} obj=0x{:08X} lr=0x{:08X}",
                ctx.r1.u32, r30_val, obj, (uint32_t)ctx.lr);
  }
  if (obj >= 0x10000u && obj < 0x80000000u) {
    const uint32_t vtbl = REX_LOAD_U32(obj);
    if (vtbl >= 0x82000000u && vtbl < 0x827C05E4u) {
      const uint32_t target_fn = REX_LOAD_U32(vtbl + 8);
      if (target_fn >= 0x821A0000u && target_fn < 0x827C05E4u) {
        ctx.r3.u64 = obj;
        ctx.lr = 0x822CACD4;
        REX_CALL_INDIRECT_FUNC(target_fn);
      }
    }
  }

  const uint32_t backchain = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r1.u32);
  if (backchain == ctx.r1.u32 + 112) {
    ctx.r1.s64 += 112;
    ctx.r12.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r1.u32 - 8);
    ctx.lr = ctx.r12.u64;
    ctx.r30.u64 = REX_LOAD_U64(ctx.r1.u32 - 24);
    ctx.r31.u64 = REX_LOAD_U64(ctx.r1.u32 - 16);
  }
}

#if !NEVERDEAD_NATIVE_CRT_MEMCPY
inline void neverdead_memcpy_8278AEEC(PPCContext& ctx, uint8_t* base) {
  uint32_t r7 = (ctx.r5.u32 >> 3) & 0xF;
  uint32_t r6 = ctx.r5.u32 & 0x7;

  // Qword copy loop (loc_8278AF04)
  for (uint32_t i = 0; i < r7; ++i) {
    ctx.r4.u32 += 8;
    ctx.r3.u32 += 8;
    uint64_t val = REX_LOAD_U64(ctx.r4.u32);
    REX_STORE_U64(ctx.r3.u32, val);
  }

  // Trailing bytes (loc_8278AF10)
  if (r6 != 0) {
    if (r6 == 4) {
      uint32_t align = ctx.r3.u32 & 0x3;
      if (align == 0) {
        uint32_t val = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r4.u32 + 8);
        REX_STORE_U32(ctx.r3.u32 + 8, val);
      } else {
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
          uint8_t b_val = REX_LOAD_U8(ctx.r4.u32 + 8 + i);
          REX_STORE_U8(ctx.r3.u32 + 8 + i, b_val);
        }
      }
    } else {
      ctx.r3.s64 += 7;
      ctx.r4.s64 += 7;
      for (uint32_t i = 0; i < r6; ++i) {
        ctx.r4.u32 += 1;
        ctx.r3.u32 += 1;
        uint8_t b_val = REX_LOAD_U8(ctx.r4.u32);
        REX_STORE_U8(ctx.r3.u32, b_val);
      }
    }
  }

  // Restore destination pointer saved at -8(r1)
  ctx.r3.u64 = REX_LOAD_U64(ctx.r1.u32 - 8);
}
#endif  // !NEVERDEAD_NATIVE_CRT_MEMCPY


=== FILE: src/nd_ke_tls_fix.h ===
// FIX(neverdead-ke-tls-set-value): override host del import xboxkrnl#0x155.
//
// Ground truth (swarm run 3, doble evidencia):
// - El thunk recompilado `sub_827BD8F4` son los bytes crudos del XEX:
//   `.long 0x1010155; .long 0x2010155; mtctr r11; bctr`. Es el stub de
//   importacion de xboxkrnl.exe ordinal 0x155 = KeTlsSetValue (vecinos
//   0x152 KeTlsAlloc en 0x827BD914, 0x154 KeTlsGetValue en 0x827BD8E4).
// - Ningun caller fija r11 (todos `mflr r12; bl` al stub via sub_8278BD48):
//   el salto va a basura (0xFFFFFBF7 / 0x827F0000, no-determinista) y muere
//   en `Call to invalid or unregistered function` (exit 0xC0000409).
// - El runtime conoce KeTls* (strings en rexruntime) pero el cuerpo
//   recompilado del stub es inalcanzable por esa via: la llamada es directa.
//
// Mecanismo (verificado en neverdead_pch.h:74-77 + rex/hook.h:49):
// DEFINE_REX_FUNC en no-Apple es alias DEBIL; REX_HOOK_RAW define el simbolo
// FUERTE con la misma firma (ctx, base) y lo sobrescribe en el link. Cubre
// las 3 vias: llamada directa `bl`, `SetFunction(0x827BD8F4, ...)` del
// registrar e indirectas por tabla (todas toman la direccion del simbolo).
// Cero toques a generated/, cero cambios al dispatcher.
//
// Semantica (funcional, no no-op: es primitiva TLS del path de arranque):
// KeTlsSetValue(index=r3, value=r4) -> r3=1 exito / 0 fallo.
// 2026-10-17 Stage B: implementado via __imp__KeTlsSetValue (rexruntime.lib).

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

#include <rex/hook.h>
#include <rex/logging.h>
#include <rex/system/xthread.h>

namespace neverdead::fix {

inline bool g_ke_tls_announced = false;

inline uint32_t PeekGuestBe32(const uint8_t* base, uint32_t guest) {
  uint32_t raw = 0;
  std::memcpy(&raw, base + guest, sizeof(raw));
  return (raw >> 24) | ((raw >> 8) & 0xFF00u) | ((raw << 8) & 0xFF0000u) |
         (raw << 24);
}

// DIAG-TEMPORAL: mini stack-walk guest (PPC: [r1]=backchain, [r1+4]=saved lr).
// Solo lectura, acotado (10 frames, backchain creciente, ventana 64KB).
inline void LogGuestStack(const uint8_t* base, uint32_t r1) {
  uint32_t cur = r1;
  for (int i = 0; i < 10 && cur != 0; ++i) {
    if (cur < 0x70000000u || cur > 0x72000000u) {
      break;
    }
    const uint32_t back = PeekGuestBe32(base, cur);
    const uint32_t lr = PeekGuestBe32(base, cur + 4);
    REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: guest-stack[{}] r1=0x{:08X} lr=0x{:08X}", i, cur,
                lr);
    if (back <= cur || back > cur + 0x10000u) {
      break;
    }
    cur = back;
  }
}

}  // namespace neverdead::fix

REX_EXTERN(__imp__KeTlsSetValue);

REX_HOOK_RAW(sub_827BD8F4) {
  const uint32_t index = ctx.r3.u32;
  const uint32_t value = ctx.r4.u32;
  if (!neverdead::fix::g_ke_tls_announced) {
    neverdead::fix::g_ke_tls_announced = true;
    REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: KeTlsSetValue override activo (xboxkrnl#0x155)");
  }
  auto* thread = rex::system::XThread::GetCurrentThread();
  // NOTA: LogGuestStack desactivado (la cima del stack guest 0x7010FF80 esta
  // junto a pagina sin mapear 0x70110000 y el peek hunde el proceso; el stack
  // host lo cubre crash_diag.cpp). Se conserva para uso futuro con
  // QueryProtect.
  (void)neverdead::fix::LogGuestStack;
  REXLOG_INFO(
      "neverdead: KeTlsSetValue ctx r1=0x{:08X} r2=0x{:08X} r13=0x{:08X} "
      "lr=0x{:08X} index=0x{:08X} value=0x{:08X}",
      (uint32_t)ctx.r1.u32, (uint32_t)ctx.r2.u32, (uint32_t)ctx.r13.u32,
      (uint32_t)ctx.lr, index, value);
  // STAGE B (2026-10-17): llamada REAL al wrapper del runtime (simbolo
  // __imp__KeTlsSetValue de rexruntime.lib, verificado con llvm-nm).
  // Semantica esperada: r3 = 1 exito / 0 fallo. Reemplaza EXPERIMENTO-A.
  (void)thread;
  (void)base;
  __imp__KeTlsSetValue(ctx, base);
}


=== FILE: src/nd_main_loop.h ===
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <rex/logging.h>
#include "nd_indirect_trap.h"

// Forward declare callees invoked by NeverDead Asura WinMain / Main Loop
DECLARE_REX_FUNC(sub_82220888);
DECLARE_REX_FUNC(sub_824DBE58);
DECLARE_REX_FUNC(sub_823C0DD0);
DECLARE_REX_FUNC(sub_823C8048);
DECLARE_REX_FUNC(sub_82788B30);
DECLARE_REX_FUNC(sub_823C9948);
DECLARE_REX_FUNC(sub_823C96E0);
DECLARE_REX_FUNC(sub_823C9E40);
DECLARE_REX_FUNC(sub_826C6C10);
DECLARE_REX_FUNC(sub_824DA078);
DECLARE_REX_FUNC(sub_8220B188);
DECLARE_REX_FUNC(sub_8278BD7C);
DECLARE_REX_FUNC(sub_8278BD80);
DECLARE_REX_FUNC(sub_8278BD84);
DECLARE_REX_FUNC(sub_8278BD88);

inline void neverdead_main_loop(PPCContext& ctx, uint8_t* base) {
  REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: Entering Asura main loop (0x823C5DAC)...");

  // FIX(neverdead-timer-init): Initialize Asura timebase frequency & ticks.
  // sub_82220888 was skipped by severed entrypoint bridge.
  // 0x828DF84C = timer frequency (50,000,000 Hz)
  // 0x82925968 = start-of-frame timebase ticks
  float* p_freq = (float*)REX_RAW_ADDR(0x828DF84C);
  if (*p_freq <= 0.0f) {
    REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: Priming Asura timebase & timer frequency via sub_82220888...");
    sub_82220888(ctx, base);
    if (*p_freq <= 0.0f) {
      *p_freq = 50000000.0f;
    }
    uint64_t* p_tb = (uint64_t*)REX_RAW_ADDR(0x82925968);
    if (*p_tb == 0) {
      *p_tb = REX_QUERY_TIMEBASE();
    }
    REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: Timer primed: freq={:.1f} Hz, start_tb=0x{:016X}", *p_freq, *p_tb);
  }

  uint8_t* p_quit = (uint8_t*)REX_RAW_ADDR(0x82B312DE);
  uint32_t* p_state = (uint32_t*)REX_RAW_ADDR(0x82B312F8);
  REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: Engine state before first frame: quit={}, state={}", *p_quit, *p_state);
  *p_quit = 0; // ensure quit flag is 0 at boot

  // 0x823C5DAC: bl 0x824dbe58
  ctx.lr = 0x823C5DB0;
  sub_824DBE58(ctx, base);

  REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: sub_824DBE58 boot check returned r3=0x{:016X} (quit={}, state={})",
              ctx.r3.u64, *p_quit, *p_state);
  if ((ctx.r3.u32 & 0xFF) == 0) {
    REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: Overriding r3=1 on boot frame to enter Asura main loop!");
    ctx.r3.u64 = 1;
  }

  // 0x823C5DB0: clrlwi r10, r3, 24
  ctx.r10.u64 = ctx.r3.u32 & 0xFF;

  // 0x823C5DB4: cmplwi cr6, r10, 0
  ctx.cr6.compare<uint32_t>(ctx.r10.u32, 0, ctx.xer);

  // 0x823C5DB8: beq cr6, 0x823c5f74
  if (ctx.cr6.eq) goto loc_823C5F74;

  // 0x823C5DBC: lis r10, -32078 (0x82B20000)
  ctx.r10.s64 = -2102263808;
  // 0x823C5DC0: lis r9, -32078
  ctx.r9.s64 = -2102263808;
  // 0x823C5DC4: lis r11, -32078
  ctx.r11.s64 = -2102263808;
  // 0x823C5DC8: lis r22, -32077 (0x82B30000)
  ctx.r22.s64 = -2102198272;
  // 0x823C5DCC: lis r28, -32078
  ctx.r28.s64 = -2102263808;
  // 0x823C5DD0: lis r27, -32078
  ctx.r27.s64 = -2102263808;
  // 0x823C5DD4: lis r26, -32078
  ctx.r26.s64 = -2102263808;

  // 0x823C5DD8: addi r25, r10, 28516 (0x6F64)
  ctx.r25.s64 = ctx.r10.s64 + 28516;
  // 0x823C5DDC: addi r24, r9, 28480 (0x6F40)
  ctx.r24.s64 = ctx.r9.s64 + 28480;
  // 0x823C5DE0: addi r23, r11, 28468 (0x6F34)
  ctx.r23.s64 = ctx.r11.s64 + 28468;

loc_823C5DE4:
  // F0 (PATCH neverdead-crt-gpr-helpers): guard de invariante de pila.
  // El marco de sub_823C5DAC es fijo (0xB0) y se reserva UNA vez en su
  // prologo; r1 no puede cambiar entre iteraciones. Si cambia, el desbordamiento
  // es aqui y no 200 lineas despues: convierte el `lr = 0xBEBEBEBE` en un
  // diagnostico localizado con el frame exacto y el delta.
  {
    static const uint32_t s_r1_base = ctx.r1.u32;
    static uint32_t s_frames = 0;
    ++s_frames;
    if (ctx.r1.u32 != s_r1_base) {
      const int32_t d = static_cast<int32_t>(ctx.r1.u32 - s_r1_base);
      if (s_frames <= 8 || (s_frames % 60) == 0) {
        REXLOG_ERROR("neverdead: STACK-DRIFT frame={} r1=0x{:08X} base=0x{:08X} delta={:+d}",
                     s_frames, ctx.r1.u32, s_r1_base, d);
      }
    } else if (s_frames <= 5 || (s_frames % 120) == 0) {
      REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: STACK-OK frame={} r1=0x{:08X}", s_frames, ctx.r1.u32);
    }
  }
  // 0x823C5DE4: bl 0x823c0dd0
  ctx.lr = 0x823C5DE8;
  sub_823C0DD0(ctx, base);

  // 0x823C5DE8: lwz r11, 28464(r26)
  ctx.r11.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r26.u32 + 28464);
  // 0x823C5DEC: li r29, 0
  ctx.r29.s64 = 0;
  // 0x823C5DF0: cmplwi cr6, r11, 0
  ctx.cr6.compare<uint32_t>(ctx.r11.u32, 0, ctx.xer);
  // 0x823C5DF4: beq cr6, 0x823c5e48
  if (ctx.cr6.eq) goto loc_823C5E48;

  // 0x823C5DF8: mr r30, r23
  ctx.r30.u64 = ctx.r23.u64;

loc_823C5DFC:
  // 0x823C5DFC: lwz r31, 0(r30)
  ctx.r31.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r30.u32 + 0);
  // 0x823C5E00: lwz r10, 152(r31) (0x98)
  ctx.r10.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r31.u32 + 152);
  // 0x823C5E04: cmplwi cr6, r10, 997 (0x3E5)
  ctx.cr6.compare<uint32_t>(ctx.r10.u32, 997, ctx.xer);
  // 0x823C5E08: beq cr6, 0x823c5e38
  if (ctx.cr6.eq) goto loc_823C5E38;

  // 0x823C5E0C: mr r3, r31
  ctx.r3.u64 = ctx.r31.u64;
  // 0x823C5E10: bl 0x823c8048
  ctx.lr = 0x823C5E14;
  sub_823C8048(ctx, base);

  // 0x823C5E14: mr r3, r31
  ctx.r3.u64 = ctx.r31.u64;
  // 0x823C5E18: bl 0x82788b30
  ctx.lr = 0x823C5E1C;
  sub_82788B30(ctx, base);

  // 0x823C5E1C: lwz r11, 28464(r26)
  ctx.r11.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r26.u32 + 28464);
  // 0x823C5E20: addi r11, r11, -1
  ctx.r11.s64 = ctx.r11.s64 - 1;
  // 0x823C5E24: rlwinm r10, r11, 2, 0, 29 (slwi r10, r11, 2)
  ctx.r10.u64 = __builtin_rotateleft64(ctx.r11.u32 | (ctx.r11.u64 << 32), 2) & 0xFFFFFFFC;
  // 0x823C5E28: stw r11, 28464(r26)
  REX_STORE_U32(ctx.r26.u32 + 28464, ctx.r11.u32);
  // 0x823C5E2C: lwzx r9, r10, r23
  ctx.r9.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r10.u32 + ctx.r23.u32);
  // 0x823C5E30: stw r9, 0(r30)
  REX_STORE_U32(ctx.r30.u32 + 0, ctx.r9.u32);
  // 0x823C5E34: b 0x823c5e40
  goto loc_823C5E40;

loc_823C5E38:
  // 0x823C5E38: addi r29, r29, 1
  ctx.r29.s64 = ctx.r29.s64 + 1;
  // 0x823C5E3C: addi r30, r30, 4
  ctx.r30.s64 = ctx.r30.s64 + 4;

loc_823C5E40:
  // 0x823C5E40: cmplw cr6, r29, r11
  ctx.cr6.compare<uint32_t>(ctx.r29.u32, ctx.r11.u32, ctx.xer);
  // 0x823C5E44: blt cr6, 0x823c5dfc
  if (ctx.cr6.lt) goto loc_823C5DFC;

loc_823C5E48:
  // 0x823C5E48: lwz r11, 28476(r27)
  ctx.r11.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r27.u32 + 28476);
  // 0x823C5E4C: li r29, 0
  ctx.r29.s64 = 0;
  // 0x823C5E50: cmplwi cr6, r11, 0
  ctx.cr6.compare<uint32_t>(ctx.r11.u32, 0, ctx.xer);
  // 0x823C5E54: beq cr6, 0x823c5eac
  if (ctx.cr6.eq) goto loc_823C5EAC;

  // 0x823C5E58: mr r31, r24
  ctx.r31.u64 = ctx.r24.u64;

loc_823C5E5C:
  // 0x823C5E5C: lwz r30, 0(r31)
  ctx.r30.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r31.u32 + 0);
  // 0x823C5E60: mr r3, r30
  ctx.r3.u64 = ctx.r30.u64;
  // 0x823C5E64: bl 0x823c9948
  ctx.lr = 0x823C5E68;
  sub_823C9948(ctx, base);

  // 0x823C5E68: lbz r11, 42(r30) (0x2A)
  ctx.r11.u64 = REX_LOAD_U8(ctx.r30.u32 + 42);
  // 0x823C5E6C: cmplwi cr6, r11, 0
  ctx.cr6.compare<uint32_t>(ctx.r11.u32, 0, ctx.xer);
  // 0x823C5E70: bne cr6, 0x823c5e98
  if (!ctx.cr6.eq) goto loc_823C5E98;

  // 0x823C5E74: mr r3, r30
  ctx.r3.u64 = ctx.r30.u64;
  // 0x823C5E78: bl 0x823c96e0
  ctx.lr = 0x823C5E7C;
  sub_823C96E0(ctx, base);

  // 0x823C5E7C: lwz r11, 28476(r27)
  ctx.r11.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r27.u32 + 28476);
  // 0x823C5E80: addi r11, r11, -1
  ctx.r11.s64 = ctx.r11.s64 - 1;
  // 0x823C5E84: rlwinm r10, r11, 2, 0, 29
  ctx.r10.u64 = __builtin_rotateleft64(ctx.r11.u32 | (ctx.r11.u64 << 32), 2) & 0xFFFFFFFC;
  // 0x823C5E88: stw r11, 28476(r27)
  REX_STORE_U32(ctx.r27.u32 + 28476, ctx.r11.u32);
  // 0x823C5E8C: lwzx r9, r10, r24
  ctx.r9.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r10.u32 + ctx.r24.u32);
  // 0x823C5E90: stw r9, 0(r31)
  REX_STORE_U32(ctx.r31.u32 + 0, ctx.r9.u32);
  // 0x823C5E94: b 0x823c5ea4
  goto loc_823C5EA4;

loc_823C5E98:
  // 0x823C5E98: lwz r11, 28476(r27)
  ctx.r11.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r27.u32 + 28476);
  // 0x823C5E9C: addi r29, r29, 1
  ctx.r29.s64 = ctx.r29.s64 + 1;
  // 0x823C5EA0: addi r31, r31, 4
  ctx.r31.s64 = ctx.r31.s64 + 4;

loc_823C5EA4:
  // 0x823C5EA4: cmplw cr6, r29, r11
  ctx.cr6.compare<uint32_t>(ctx.r29.u32, ctx.r11.u32, ctx.xer);
  // 0x823C5EA8: blt cr6, 0x823c5e5c
  if (ctx.cr6.lt) goto loc_823C5E5C;

loc_823C5EAC:
  // 0x823C5EAC: lwz r11, 28512(r28)
  ctx.r11.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r28.u32 + 28512);
  // 0x823C5EB0: li r29, 0
  ctx.r29.s64 = 0;
  // 0x823C5EB4: cmplwi cr6, r11, 0
  ctx.cr6.compare<uint32_t>(ctx.r11.u32, 0, ctx.xer);
  // 0x823C5EB8: beq cr6, 0x823c5f64
  if (ctx.cr6.eq) goto loc_823C5F64;

  // 0x823C5EBC: mr r30, r25
  ctx.r30.u64 = ctx.r25.u64;

loc_823C5EC0:
  // 0x823C5EC0: lwz r31, 0(r30)
  ctx.r31.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r30.u32 + 0);
  // 0x823C5EC4: lwz r10, 8(r31)
  ctx.r10.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r31.u32 + 8);
  // 0x823C5EC8: cmplwi cr6, r10, 0
  ctx.cr6.compare<uint32_t>(ctx.r10.u32, 0, ctx.xer);
  // 0x823C5ECC: beq cr6, 0x823c5f0c
  if (ctx.cr6.eq) goto loc_823C5F0C;

  // 0x823C5ED0: rotlwi r3, r10, 0
  ctx.r3.u64 = __builtin_rotateleft32(ctx.r10.u32, 0);
  // 0x823C5ED4: lbz r21, 40(r3) (0x28)
  ctx.r21.u64 = REX_LOAD_U8(ctx.r3.u32 + 40);
  // 0x823C5ED8: bl 0x823c9948
  ctx.lr = 0x823C5EDC;
  sub_823C9948(ctx, base);

  // 0x823C5EDC: lwz r10, 8(r31)
  ctx.r10.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r31.u32 + 8);
  // 0x823C5EE0: mr r11, r21
  ctx.r11.u64 = ctx.r21.u64;
  // 0x823C5EE4: lbz r9, 40(r10) (0x28)
  ctx.r9.u64 = REX_LOAD_U8(ctx.r10.u32 + 40);
  // 0x823C5EE8: cmplw cr6, r21, r9
  ctx.cr6.compare<uint32_t>(ctx.r21.u32, ctx.r9.u32, ctx.xer);
  // 0x823C5EEC: beq cr6, 0x823c5f08
  if (ctx.cr6.eq) goto loc_823C5F08;

  // 0x823C5EF0: lwz r11, 13200(r22) (0x3390)
  ctx.r11.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r22.u32 + 13200);
  // 0x823C5EF4: cmplwi cr6, r11, 0
  ctx.cr6.compare<uint32_t>(ctx.r11.u32, 0, ctx.xer);
  // 0x823C5EF8: beq cr6, 0x823c5f08
  if (ctx.cr6.eq) goto loc_823C5F08;

  // 0x823C5EFC: rotlwi r11, r11, 0
  ctx.r11.u64 = __builtin_rotateleft32(ctx.r11.u32, 0);
  // 0x823C5F00: mtctr r11
  ctx.ctr.u64 = ctx.r11.u64;
  // 0x823C5F04: bctrl
  ctx.lr = 0x823C5F08;
  REX_CALL_INDIRECT_FUNC(ctx.ctr.u32);

loc_823C5F08:
  // 0x823C5F08: lwz r11, 28512(r28)
  ctx.r11.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r28.u32 + 28512);

loc_823C5F0C:
  // 0x823C5F0C: lwz r10, 8(r31)
  ctx.r10.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r31.u32 + 8);
  // 0x823C5F10: cmplwi cr6, r10, 0
  ctx.cr6.compare<uint32_t>(ctx.r10.u32, 0, ctx.xer);
  // 0x823C5F14: beq cr6, 0x823c5f20
  if (ctx.cr6.eq) goto loc_823C5F20;

  // 0x823C5F18: lbz r10, 42(r10) (0x2A)
  ctx.r10.u64 = REX_LOAD_U8(ctx.r10.u32 + 42);
  // 0x823C5F1C: b 0x823c5f24
  goto loc_823C5F24;

loc_823C5F20:
  // 0x823C5F20: li r10, 0
  ctx.r10.s64 = 0;

loc_823C5F24:
  // 0x823C5F24: clrlwi r10, r10, 24
  ctx.r10.u64 = ctx.r10.u32 & 0xFF;
  // 0x823C5F28: cmplwi cr6, r10, 0
  ctx.cr6.compare<uint32_t>(ctx.r10.u32, 0, ctx.xer);
  // 0x823C5F2C: bne cr6, 0x823c5f54
  if (!ctx.cr6.eq) goto loc_823C5F54;

  // 0x823C5F30: mr r3, r31
  ctx.r3.u64 = ctx.r31.u64;
  // 0x823C5F34: bl 0x823c9e40
  ctx.lr = 0x823C5F38;
  sub_823C9E40(ctx, base);

  // 0x823C5F38: lwz r11, 28512(r28)
  ctx.r11.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r28.u32 + 28512);
  // 0x823C5F3C: addi r11, r11, -1
  ctx.r11.s64 = ctx.r11.s64 - 1;
  // 0x823C5F40: rlwinm r10, r11, 2, 0, 29
  ctx.r10.u64 = __builtin_rotateleft64(ctx.r11.u32 | (ctx.r11.u64 << 32), 2) & 0xFFFFFFFC;
  // 0x823C5F44: stw r11, 28512(r28)
  REX_STORE_U32(ctx.r28.u32 + 28512, ctx.r11.u32);
  // 0x823C5F48: lwzx r9, r10, r25
  ctx.r9.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r10.u32 + ctx.r25.u32);
  // 0x823C5F4C: stw r9, 0(r30)
  REX_STORE_U32(ctx.r30.u32 + 0, ctx.r9.u32);
  // 0x823C5F50: b 0x823c5f5c
  goto loc_823C5F5C;

loc_823C5F54:
  // 0x823C5F54: addi r29, r29, 1
  ctx.r29.s64 = ctx.r29.s64 + 1;
  // 0x823C5F58: addi r30, r30, 4
  ctx.r30.s64 = ctx.r30.s64 + 4;

loc_823C5F5C:
  // 0x823C5F5C: cmplw cr6, r29, r11
  ctx.cr6.compare<uint32_t>(ctx.r29.u32, ctx.r11.u32, ctx.xer);
  // 0x823C5F60: blt cr6, 0x823c5ec0
  if (ctx.cr6.lt) goto loc_823C5EC0;

loc_823C5F64:
  // 0x823C5F64: bl 0x824dbe58
  ctx.lr = 0x823C5F68;
  sub_824DBE58(ctx, base);
  {
    static uint32_t s_frame_count = 0;
    if (++s_frame_count <= 20 || s_frame_count % 60 == 0) {
      REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: Frame #{} sub_824DBE58 returned r3=0x{:016X} (quit={}, state={})",
                  s_frame_count, ctx.r3.u64, *p_quit, *p_state);
    }
  }

  // 0x823C5F68: clrlwi r11, r3, 24
  ctx.r11.u64 = ctx.r3.u32 & 0xFF;
  // 0x823C5F6C: cmplwi cr6, r11, 0
  ctx.cr6.compare<uint32_t>(ctx.r11.u32, 0, ctx.xer);
  // 0x823C5F70: bne cr6, 0x823c5de4
  if (!ctx.cr6.eq) goto loc_823C5DE4;

loc_823C5F74:
  REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: Asura engine shutting down (0x823C5F74)...");
  // 0x823C5F74: lis r11, -32077
  ctx.r11.s64 = -2102198272;
  // 0x823C5F78: lwz r3, 15944(r11) (0x3E48)
  ctx.r3.u64 = REX_LOAD_U32(ctx.r11.u32 + 15944);
  // 0x823C5F7C: bl 0x826c6c10
  ctx.lr = 0x823C5F80;
  sub_826C6C10(ctx, base);
  // 0x823C5F80: bl 0x824da078
  ctx.lr = 0x823C5F84;
  sub_824DA078(ctx, base);
  // 0x823C5F84: bl 0x8220b188
  ctx.lr = 0x823C5F88;
  sub_8220B188(ctx, base);
  // 0x823C5F88: li r3, 0
  ctx.r3.s64 = 0;
  // 0x823C5F8C: addi r1, r1, 176 (0xB0)
  ctx.r1.s64 = ctx.r1.s64 + 176;
  // 0x823C5F90: b 0x8278bd7c
  //
  // CORRECCION (PATCH neverdead-crt-gpr-helpers): 0x8278BD7C NO es un fragmento
  // de cola de epilogo compartida; es `__restgprlr_21`
  // (ld r21..r31 desde -96..-16(r1) ; lwz r12,-8(r1) ; mtlr r12 ; blr), y es
  // exactamente la cola que el invitado necesita aqui: sub_823C5DAC tiene marco
  // 0xB0 y salva r21..r31, asi que `addi r1,+0xB0` seguido de `b 0x8278BD7C`
  // restaura el marco y devuelve por el LR del llamante.
  //
  // La version anterior encadenaba sub_8278BD7C/80/84/88 a mano porque creia
  // que eran fragmentos de una cola; con el cuerpo canonico eso restauraria
  // cuatro juegos de registros distintos y volveria por un LR equivocado.
  sub_8278BD7C(ctx, base);
  return;
}


=== FILE: src/neverdead_app.h ===
// neverdead - ReXGlue Recompiled Project
//
// Customize your app by overriding virtual hooks from rex::ReXApp.

#pragma once

#include <bit>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <string>

#ifdef _WIN32
#ifndef NOMINMAX
#define NOMINMAX
#endif
#ifndef WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#define WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#endif
#include <windows.h>
#endif

#include <rex/logging.h>
#include <rex/rex_app.h>
#include <rex/filesystem/vfs.h>

#include "nd_import_diag.h"
#include "nd_ke_tls_fix.h"
#include "nd_hooks.h"

class NeverdeadApp : public rex::ReXApp {
 public:
  using rex::ReXApp::ReXApp;

  static std::unique_ptr<rex::ui::WindowedApp> Create(
      rex::ui::WindowedAppContext& ctx) {
    return std::unique_ptr<NeverdeadApp>(new NeverdeadApp(ctx, "neverdead",
        PPCImageConfig));
  }

  // FIX(neverdead-xex-image): sin este override el XEX nunca se vuelca a la
  // arena guest y .rdata/.data quedan sin mapear: el slot en 0x82144940 lee
  // 0xFFFFFBFF y loc_824D9E94 muere en `Call to invalid or unregistered
  // function` (exit 0xC0000409). Fijar el alias esperado del asset basta; el
  // SDK mapea las secciones desde default.xex (9.5MB en assets/).
  void OnLoadXexImage(std::string& xex_image) override {
    xex_image = "game:\\default.xex";
  }

  // DIAG(neverdead-xex-probe): verifica tras la carga que el slot que mata al
  // arranque ya contiene la VTABLE real. Solo diagnostica: no parchea memoria
  // (si el SDK ya mapea .rdata/.data este log sale OK y no hace falta tocar
  // OnPreLaunchModule).
  void MapBaseExeSections() {
    auto* rt = runtime();
    if (rt == nullptr || rt->memory() == nullptr) return;
    auto* mem = rt->memory();
    uint8_t* guest_base = mem->TranslateVirtual<uint8_t*>(0x82000000u);
    if (!guest_base) {
      REXLOG_ERROR("neverdead: MapBaseExeSections cannot translate guest 0x82000000");
      return;
    }

#ifdef _WIN32
    // CRITICO: Desbloquear permisos de escritura en cada region de [0x82000000, 0x82C10000)
    // iterando via VirtualQuery (porque VirtualProtect falla si el rango cruza multiples regiones MBI).
    {
      uint8_t* curr = guest_base;
      uint8_t* end = guest_base + 0x00C10000u;
      size_t count = 0;
      while (curr < end) {
        MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION mbi{};
        if (!VirtualQuery(curr, &mbi, sizeof(mbi))) break;
        if (mbi.State == MEM_COMMIT && mbi.Protect != PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE && mbi.Protect != PAGE_READWRITE) {
          DWORD old_prot = 0;
          DWORD target_prot = (mbi.AllocationProtect & 0xF0) ? PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE : PAGE_READWRITE;
          if (VirtualProtect(mbi.BaseAddress, mbi.RegionSize, target_prot, &old_prot)) {
            ++count;
          } else if (VirtualProtect(mbi.BaseAddress, mbi.RegionSize, PAGE_READWRITE, &old_prot)) {
            ++count;
          }
        }
        curr = reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(mbi.BaseAddress) + mbi.RegionSize;
      }
      REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: MapBaseExeSections unlocked {} memory regions to writable", count);
    }
#endif

    auto exe_dir = rex::filesystem::GetExecutableFolder();
    auto try_open = [](const std::filesystem::path& p) -> FILE* {
      std::error_code ec;
      if (std::filesystem::is_regular_file(p, ec)) {
        return fopen(p.string().c_str(), "rb");
      }
      return nullptr;
    };
    FILE* f = try_open(exe_dir.parent_path() / "NeverDead_base.bin");
    if (!f) f = try_open(exe_dir / "NeverDead_base.bin");
    if (!f) f = try_open(exe_dir.parent_path() / "assets" / "NeverDead_base.bin");
    if (!f) f = try_open(exe_dir / "assets" / "NeverDead_base.bin");
    if (!f) f = try_open("NeverDead_base.bin");
    if (!f) f = try_open("../NeverDead_base.bin");
    if (!f) {
      REXLOG_WARN("neverdead: MapBaseExeSections: NeverDead_base.bin no encontrado (no critico si default.xex mapea .data)");
      return;
    }

    // PATCH(neverdead-rdata-holes): rellenar HUECOS, no sobrescribir.
    //
    // EVIDENCIA (sonda pre-map, neverdead_012.log): en la imagen que carga el XEX,
    //   0x82144930[+0]  = 0x822CE8F0   (vtable valida)
    //   0x82144930[+16] = 0x824DDF90   (puntero a funcion valido)
    //   0x82144930[+32] = 0x824DDFF0   (puntero a funcion valido)
    // Tras la copia 1:1 del .exe, [+16] pasaba a 0x006C0065 = "le" (texto UTF-16).
    //
    // CORRECCION TRAS AUDITORIA ADVERSARIA (2026-10-23): el desplazamiento entre el
    // .exe y la imagen guest NO es constante. Medido con anclas de contenido
    // (volcados `dump` del log, comparados contra el .exe):
    //
    //   .data   rva 0x7DBFA8 / 0x7EA280 / 0x7EA2B0  -> shift 0x0000  (3/3 anclas)
    //   .rdata  rva 0x04AFC0 / 0x04D350 / 0x04D360  -> shift 0x0000  (3/3 anclas)
    //   .rdata  rva 0x144930                          -> shift 0x1000  (5/5 palabras)
    //   .rdata  rva 0x49EF98 / 0x49EFA8               -> shift 0x2000  (2/2 anclas)
    //
    // Es decir: dentro de la MISMA seccion .rdata el desplazamiento toma tres
    // valores distintos. No existe un offset fijo por seccion que sea correcto,
    // asi que inyectar con offset fijo es inyectar basura en parte de los huecos.
    //
    // Regla nueva (conservadora y verificable):
    //   * .data  -> se rellena con offset = rva (shift 0 verificado).
    //   * .rdata -> NO se inyecta: el loader del XEX la carga correctamente
    //               (lo demuestra la sonda pre-map: los punteros ya eran validos
    //               antes de tocar nada) y su offset correcto no es conocible.
    //   * .pdata -> no se inyecta (rellenaba 0 palabras de todos modos).
    //   * .post  -> no se inyecta: el rango 0xB7BE00..0xB90200 cae mas alla de la
    //               ultima seccion del .exe (0x8EDC00), lee cola de fichero.
    //   * ND_FILL_RDATA=1 / ND_FILL_POST=1 reactivan las dos ultimas para A/B.
    auto env_on = [](const char* name) {
      const char* v = std::getenv(name);
      return v != nullptr && v[0] != '\0' && v[0] != '0';
    };
    auto fill_holes = [&](const char* tag, uint32_t rva, uint32_t size,
                          uint32_t shift, bool enabled) -> uint32_t {
      if (!enabled) {
        REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: MapBaseExeSections {} rva=0x{:X} size=0x{:X} DESACTIVADO",
                    tag, rva, size);
        return 0;
      }
      static uint8_t buf[0x10000];
      uint32_t filled = 0;
      uint32_t done = 0;
      while (done < size) {
        const uint32_t chunk = (size - done) < sizeof(buf) ? (size - done) : (uint32_t)sizeof(buf);
        if (fseek(f, (long)(rva + done - shift), SEEK_SET) != 0) break;
        const size_t got = fread(buf, 1, chunk, f);
        if (got == 0) break;
        uint8_t* dst = guest_base + rva + done;
        for (size_t i = 0; i + 4 <= got; i += 4) {
          uint32_t g, s;
          std::memcpy(&g, dst + i, 4);
          std::memcpy(&s, buf + i, 4);
          if (g == 0 && s != 0) {
            std::memcpy(dst + i, buf + i, 4);
            ++filled;
          }
        }
        done += (uint32_t)got;
        if (got < chunk) break;
      }
      REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: MapBaseExeSections {} rva=0x{:X} size=0x{:X} shift=0x{:X} palabras_rellenadas={}",
                  tag, rva, size, shift, filled);
      return filled;
    };

    const uint32_t h1 = fill_holes(".rdata", 0x00000A40u, 0x0016F800u - 0x00000A40u,
                                   0x0000u, env_on("ND_FILL_RDATA"));
    const uint32_t h2 = fill_holes(".pdata", 0x0016F800u, 0x00195E00u - 0x0016F800u,
                                   0x0000u, false);
    const uint32_t h3 = fill_holes(".data", 0x007D5C00u, 0x000C1200u,
                                   0x0000u, true);
    const uint32_t h4 = fill_holes(".post", 0x00B7BE00u, 0x00B90200u - 0x00B7BE00u,
                                   0x0000u, env_on("ND_FILL_POST"));

    fclose(f);
    REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: MapBaseExeSections holes-only completado "
                "(.rdata={}, .pdata={}, .data={}, .post={}; IAT [0x600,0xA40) preservado)",
                h1, h2, h3, h4);
  }

  void SetupGuestMemoryHooks() {
    auto* rt = runtime();
    if (rt == nullptr || rt->memory() == nullptr) return;
    auto* mem = rt->memory();
    nd_guest_mem_set(static_cast<void*>(mem));
    nd_set_translate(&nd_translate_va);
  }

  void OnPostLoadXexImage() override {
    auto* rt = runtime();
    if (rt == nullptr || rt->memory() == nullptr) {
      return;
    }
    // DIAG(pre-map): que contiene la imagen guest del XEX ANTES de que
    // MapBaseExeSections copie encima el NeverDead_base.exe.  Sirve para
    // separar "el XEX tiene basura aqui" de "el exe compactado la introduce".
    if (auto* pre_mem = rt->memory()) {
      const uint32_t va = 0x82144930u;
      if (const auto* p = pre_mem->TranslateVirtual<const uint8_t*>(va)) {
        uint32_t w0, w1, w2, w3, w4;
        std::memcpy(&w0, p + 0x00, 4);
        std::memcpy(&w1, p + 0x10, 4);
        std::memcpy(&w2, p + 0x20, 4);
        std::memcpy(&w3, p + 0x30, 4);
        std::memcpy(&w4, p + 0x40, 4);
        REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: pre-map obj@82144930 [+0]={:08X} [+16]={:08X} [+32]={:08X} [+48]={:08X} [+64]={:08X}",
                    std::byteswap(w0), std::byteswap(w1), std::byteswap(w2),
                    std::byteswap(w3), std::byteswap(w4));
      }
    }
    MapBaseExeSections();
    auto* mem = rt->memory();
    constexpr uint32_t kObjBase = 0x82144930u;
    constexpr uint32_t kSlot = kObjBase + 16u;  // loc_824D9E94 lee guest[kSlot]
    constexpr uint32_t kExpectVtable = 0x8249EF98u;  // VTABLE fisica en XEX RVA 0x144940
    const uint32_t raw = *mem->TranslateVirtual<const uint32_t*>(kSlot);
    // La arena guest es big-endian vista desde el host little-endian.
    const uint32_t slot = std::byteswap(raw);
    REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: post-load probe obj=0x{:08X} slot=0x{:08X} value=0x{:08X}",
                kObjBase, kSlot, slot);
    if (slot == kExpectVtable) {
      REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: .rdata OK (VTABLE 0x{:08X} presente), loc_824D9E94 debe avanzar a GUIMenu",
                  kExpectVtable);
    } else {
      REXLOG_WARN("neverdead: .rdata ausente en slot (got 0x{:08X}, want 0x{:08X}); revisar game_data_root/XEX",
                  slot, kExpectVtable);
    }
    constexpr uint32_t kVtableAddr = 0x82140958u;
    const uint32_t v0 = std::byteswap(*mem->TranslateVirtual<const uint32_t*>(kVtableAddr));
    const uint32_t v1 = std::byteswap(*mem->TranslateVirtual<const uint32_t*>(kVtableAddr + 4));
    REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: post-load vtable@82140958 = [0x{:08X}, 0x{:08X}]", v0, v1);
    if (v0 == 0x8249EF98u && v1 == 0x825C3370u) {
      REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: .rdata OK! Real VTABLE 0x8249EF98 confirmed active at 0x82140958");
    }

    // DIAG(map-shift): escaneo la imagen guest buscando anchors unicos del
    // archivo (deben estar byteswapeados: arena guest BE vista host LE).
    // Mide el desfase real runtime<->archivo por seccion (.rdata y .text).
    struct Anchor {
      uint32_t word0;
      uint32_t word1;
      const char* tag;
    };
    constexpr uint32_t kImageBase = 0x82000000u;
    constexpr uint32_t kImageSize = 0xA10000u;
    const Anchor anchors[] = {
        {0x8249EF98u, 0x825C3370u, "vtable@82144940"},  // .rdata RVA 0x144940
        {0x7D8802A6u, 0x48588A95u, "ctor@822032B0"},   // .text RVA 0x1FE2B0
        {0x7D8802A6u, 0x482B1F51u, "entry@824D9DF8"},  // .text RVA 0x4D4DF8
        {0x48031BADu, 0x48000014u, "stub@8278BD48"},   // .text RVA 0x786D48
    };
    const uint32_t* base = mem->TranslateVirtual<const uint32_t*>(kImageBase);
    if (base == nullptr) {
      REXLOG_ERROR("neverdead: map-diag no puedeTraslate 0x{:08X}", kImageBase);
      return;
    }
    const size_t words = kImageSize / sizeof(uint32_t);
    for (const auto& a : anchors) {
      const uint32_t w0 = std::byteswap(a.word0);
      const uint32_t w1 = std::byteswap(a.word1);
      uint32_t matches = 0;
      for (size_t i = 0; i + 1 < words && matches < 4; ++i) {
        if (base[i] == w0 && base[i + 1] == w1) {
          const uint32_t guest = kImageBase + static_cast<uint32_t>(i * 4);
          REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: map-diag {} host-idx={:08X} guest=0x{:08X}",
                      a.tag, (uint32_t)i, guest);
          ++matches;
        }
      }
      if (matches == 0) {
        REXLOG_WARN("neverdead: map-diag {} SIN MATCH en imagen guest", a.tag);
      }
    }

    // DIAG(raw-dump): regiones clave del flujo de aborto (2026-10-17).
    auto dump = [&](const char* tag, uint32_t va, uint32_t nbytes) {
      const auto* p = mem->TranslateVirtual<const uint8_t*>(va);
      if (p == nullptr) {
        REXLOG_WARN("neverdead: dump {} 0x{:08X} no traducible", tag, va);
        return;
      }
      for (uint32_t off = 0; off < nbytes; off += 16) {
        uint32_t w0, w1, w2, w3;
        std::memcpy(&w0, p + off, 4);
        std::memcpy(&w1, p + off + 4, 4);
        std::memcpy(&w2, p + off + 8, 4);
        std::memcpy(&w3, p + off + 12, 4);
        REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: dump {} 0x{:08X}: {:08X} {:08X} {:08X} {:08X}",
                    tag, va + off, std::byteswap(w0), std::byteswap(w1),
                    std::byteswap(w2), std::byteswap(w3));
      }
    };
    dump("dtor-array", 0x827DBF90u, 0x70u);
    dump("cs-table", 0x827EA280u, 0x80u);
    dump("vtbl-ptr", 0x8249EF98u, 0x20u);
    dump("name-pool", 0x8204AFC0u, 0x40u);
    dump("cs-pool", 0x8204D340u, 0x40u);

    // FIX(neverdead-rdata-write): Xbox 360 no impone proteccion de solo lectura
    // en .rdata; motores como Asura engine modifican tablas globales en .rdata (0x82080004).
    // Asegurar que cada region comprometida en [0x82000000, 0x82C10000) sea PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE.
#ifdef _WIN32
    void* host_img = mem->TranslateVirtual<void*>(0x82000000u);
    if (host_img != nullptr) {
      uint8_t* curr = reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(host_img);
      uint8_t* end = curr + 0x00C10000u;
      size_t count = 0;
      while (curr < end) {
        MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION mbi{};
        if (!VirtualQuery(curr, &mbi, sizeof(mbi))) break;
        REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: MBI base={:p} alloc_base={:p} alloc_prot=0x{:X} size=0x{:X} state=0x{:X} prot=0x{:X} type=0x{:X}",
                    mbi.BaseAddress, mbi.AllocationBase, mbi.AllocationProtect,
                    (size_t)mbi.RegionSize, mbi.State, mbi.Protect, mbi.Type);
        if (mbi.State == MEM_COMMIT && mbi.Protect != PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE && mbi.Protect != PAGE_READWRITE) {
          DWORD old_prot = 0;
          DWORD target_prot = (mbi.AllocationProtect & 0xF0) ? PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE : PAGE_READWRITE;
          if (VirtualProtect(mbi.BaseAddress, mbi.RegionSize, target_prot, &old_prot)) {
            REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: region {:p} (size 0x{:X}) changed 0x{:X} -> 0x{:X}",
                        mbi.BaseAddress, (size_t)mbi.RegionSize, old_prot, target_prot);
            ++count;
          } else {
            DWORD err1 = GetLastError();
            if (VirtualProtect(mbi.BaseAddress, mbi.RegionSize, PAGE_READWRITE, &old_prot)) {
              REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: region {:p} (size 0x{:X}) changed 0x{:X} -> PAGE_READWRITE",
                          mbi.BaseAddress, (size_t)mbi.RegionSize, old_prot);
              ++count;
            } else {
              REXLOG_ERROR("neverdead: VirtualProtect {:p} failed: err1={} err2={}", mbi.BaseAddress, err1, GetLastError());
            }
          }
        }
        curr = reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(mbi.BaseAddress) + mbi.RegionSize;
      }
      REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: total {} regions updated to writable", count);
    }

    // FIX(neverdead-guest-ram): Pre-commit 1GB guest physical RAM + WC mirror [0x00000000, 0x40000000)
    // Eliminates 86,000+ VEH page faults and disk I/O bottlenecks during Asura engine boot memory initialization.
    void* host_ram = mem->TranslateVirtual<void*>(0x00000000u);
    if (host_ram != nullptr) {
      void* allocated = VirtualAlloc(host_ram, 0x40000000, MEM_COMMIT, PAGE_READWRITE);
      DWORD err = allocated ? 0 : GetLastError();
      REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: guest physical RAM pre-commit [0x00000000, 0x40000000) at {:p} result={:p} (err={})",
                  host_ram, allocated, err);
    }

    void* host_mid = mem->TranslateVirtual<void*>(0x40000000u);
    if (host_mid != nullptr) {
      void* allocated = VirtualAlloc(host_mid, 0x40000000, MEM_COMMIT, PAGE_READWRITE);
      DWORD err = allocated ? 0 : GetLastError();
      REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: guest mid/high memory pre-commit [0x40000000, 0x80000000) at {:p} result={:p} (err={})",
                  host_mid, allocated, err);
    }

    // FIX(neverdead-physical-arena): Pre-reserve & commit physical_membase [0x0000000300000000, 0x0000000380000000) (2GB)
    // Prevents rexgpu-xenos command processor reading beyond uncommitted physical bounds.
    void* host_phys = mem->physical_membase();
    if (host_phys != nullptr) {
      void* allocated = VirtualAlloc(host_phys, 0x80000000, MEM_RESERVE | MEM_COMMIT, PAGE_READWRITE);
      if (!allocated) {
        allocated = VirtualAlloc(host_phys, 0x20010000, MEM_RESERVE | MEM_COMMIT, PAGE_READWRITE);
      }
      if (!allocated) {
        allocated = VirtualAlloc(host_phys, 0x20010000, MEM_COMMIT, PAGE_READWRITE);
      }
      DWORD err = allocated ? 0 : GetLastError();
      REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: physical memory arena pre-commit at {:p} result={:p} (err={})",
                  host_phys, allocated, err);
    }

    // FIX(neverdead-stacks): Pre-commit 240MB of guest stack and user heap memory [0x70000000, 0x7F000000)
    // Eliminates 23,800+ VEH page faults during Asura engine boot memory initialization.
    void* host_stacks = mem->TranslateVirtual<void*>(0x70000000u);
    if (host_stacks != nullptr) {
      void* allocated = VirtualAlloc(host_stacks, 0x0F000000, MEM_COMMIT, PAGE_READWRITE);
      DWORD err = allocated ? 0 : GetLastError();
      REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: guest stacks/user heap commit at {:p} (240MB) result={:p} (err={})", host_stacks, allocated, err);
    }

    // FIX(neverdead-pre-image-heap): Pre-commit 32MB pre-image heap [0x80000000, 0x82000000)
    void* host_pre_img = mem->TranslateVirtual<void*>(0x80000000u);
    if (host_pre_img != nullptr) {
      void* allocated = VirtualAlloc(host_pre_img, 0x02000000, MEM_COMMIT, PAGE_READWRITE);
      DWORD err = allocated ? 0 : GetLastError();
      REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: pre-image heap commit [0x80000000, 0x82000000) at {:p} result={:p} (err={})",
                  host_pre_img, allocated, err);
    }

    // NOTA CRITICA VTABLES: [0x82000000, 0x82C10000) es la imagen XEX (.text y .rdata/vtables).
    // NO invocar VirtualAlloc aqui para no revocar PAGE_EXECUTE. Mantenido como PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE.

    // FIX(neverdead-post-image-heap): Pre-commit post-image heap in view v80000000 [0x82C10000, 0x90000000) (~211.9MB)
    void* host_post_img = mem->TranslateVirtual<void*>(0x82C10000u);
    if (host_post_img != nullptr) {
      void* allocated = VirtualAlloc(host_post_img, 0x0D3F0000, MEM_COMMIT, PAGE_READWRITE);
      DWORD err = allocated ? 0 : GetLastError();
      REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: post-image heap commit [0x82C10000, 0x90000000) at {:p} result={:p} (err={})",
                  host_post_img, allocated, err);
    }

    // FIX(neverdead-v90-kernel-heap): Pre-commit extended kernel heap in view v90000000 [0x90000000, 0x92000000) (32MB)
    void* host_v90 = mem->TranslateVirtual<void*>(0x90000000u);
    if (host_v90 != nullptr) {
      void* allocated = VirtualAlloc(host_v90, 0x02000000, MEM_COMMIT, PAGE_READWRITE);
      DWORD err = allocated ? 0 : GetLastError();
      REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: extended kernel heap commit [0x90000000, 0x92000000) at {:p} result={:p} (err={})",
                  host_v90, allocated, err);
    }

    // MBI safety sweep: ensures no uncommitted gaps remain in physical RAM and kernel heap
    auto ensure_range_committed = [&](uint32_t guest_start, uint32_t size, const char* name) {
      void* host_start = mem->TranslateVirtual<void*>(guest_start);
      if (!host_start) return;
      uint8_t* curr = reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(host_start);
      uint8_t* end = curr + size;
      size_t committed_bytes = 0, reserved_fixed = 0;
      while (curr < end) {
        MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION mbi{};
        if (!VirtualQuery(curr, &mbi, sizeof(mbi))) break;
        uint8_t* r_start = reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(mbi.BaseAddress);
        size_t r_size = mbi.RegionSize;
        if (r_start < curr) {
          r_size -= (curr - r_start);
          r_start = curr;
        }
        if (r_start + r_size > end) {
          r_size = end - r_start;
        }
        if (mbi.State == MEM_RESERVE) {
          void* res = VirtualAlloc(r_start, r_size, MEM_COMMIT, PAGE_READWRITE);
          if (res) reserved_fixed += r_size;
        } else if (mbi.State == MEM_COMMIT) {
          committed_bytes += r_size;
        }
        curr = reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(mbi.BaseAddress) + mbi.RegionSize;
      }
      REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: {} [0x{:08X}, 0x{:08X}) verified committed=0x{:X} newly_fixed=0x{:X}",
                  name, guest_start, guest_start + size, committed_bytes, reserved_fixed);
    };
    ensure_range_committed(0x00000000u, 0x20000000u, "guest physical RAM");
    ensure_range_committed(0x40000000u, 0x40000000u, "guest mid/high RAM");
    ensure_range_committed(0x70000000u, 0x0F000000u, "guest stacks/user heap");
    ensure_range_committed(0x80000000u, 0x02000000u, "pre-image heap");
    ensure_range_committed(0x82C10000u, 0x0F3F0000u, "post-image kernel heap");
#endif

    // Parche de 60 FPS (Xenia "Unlock FPS"). Debe ir despues del mapeo para
    // que ninguna copia posterior lo sobrescriba.
    SetupGuestMemoryHooks();
  }


  void OnConfigurePaths(rex::PathConfig& paths) override {
    auto exe_dir = rex::filesystem::GetExecutableFolder();
    auto root_dir = exe_dir.parent_path();
    std::error_code ec;

    // 1. Resolver game_data_root si esta vacio o si el path relativo "assets" no existe en el CWD
    if (paths.game_data_root.empty() || !std::filesystem::exists(paths.game_data_root, ec)) {
      auto candidate = exe_dir / "assets";
      if (std::filesystem::is_directory(candidate, ec)) {
        paths.game_data_root = candidate;
        REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: auto-configured game_data_root from exe_dir: {}", candidate.string());
      } else {
        auto parent_candidate = root_dir / "assets";
        if (std::filesystem::is_directory(parent_candidate, ec)) {
          paths.game_data_root = parent_candidate;
          REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: auto-configured game_data_root from parent dir: {}", parent_candidate.string());
        }
      }
    }

    // 2. Unificar neverdead.toml: si existe en la raiz del proyecto, usarlo como fuente unica de verdad
    auto root_toml = root_dir / "neverdead.toml";
    if (std::filesystem::is_regular_file(root_toml, ec)) {
      paths.config_path = root_toml;
      REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: unified config_path to project root: {}", root_toml.string());
    }

    // 3. Auto-configurar cache_root persistente en la raiz del proyecto si no viene fijado por CLI o no existe
    if (paths.cache_root.empty() || !std::filesystem::exists(paths.cache_root, ec)) {
      auto root_cache = root_dir / "cache";
      std::filesystem::create_directories(root_cache, ec);
      paths.cache_root = root_cache;
      REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: auto-configured cache_root to: {}", root_cache.string());
    }
  }

  void OnPreSetup(rex::RuntimeConfig& config) override {
    config.gpu_plugin = "xenos";
    // CRITICO (GATE 1): instalar ANTES de que se cree el Presenter/ventana.
    // El hook de SetPaintModeFromUIThread fuerza paint_mode_=2 en la primera
    // transicion, de modo que el Presenter no nace en kUIThreadOnRequest. El
    // hook de DXGI Present se instala tambien aqui para enganchar el swap
    // chain en cuanto IDXGIFactory2::CreateSwapChainForHwnd lo cree.
    NdEarlyInit();
    REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: configured gpu_plugin = 'xenos' for D3D12 presentation");
  }

  void OnPostSetup() override {
    // Reintento idempotente: rexgpu-xenos.dll puede cargarse durante Setup, y
    // el TOML/cvar de render_target_path se lee en la inicializacion GPU.
    NdEarlyInit();
    auto* rt = runtime();
    if (rt != nullptr && rt->file_system() != nullptr) {
      auto* fs = rt->file_system();
      std::string game_target;
      if (fs->FindSymbolicLink("game:", game_target)) {
        REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: VFS game: mapped to '{}'", game_target);
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("dvd:", game_target);
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("cdrom0:", game_target);
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("assets:", game_target);
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("FMV:", game_target + "\\FMV");
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("GUIMenu:", game_target + "\\GUIMenu");
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("Fonts:", game_target + "\\Fonts");
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("Text:", game_target + "\\Text");
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("misc:", game_target + "\\misc");
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("sounds:", game_target + "\\sounds");
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("D:", game_target);
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("d:", game_target);
      } else {
        std::string fallback = "\\Device\\Harddisk0\\Partition1";
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("dvd:", fallback);
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("cdrom0:", fallback);
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("assets:", fallback);
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("FMV:", fallback + "\\FMV");
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("GUIMenu:", fallback + "\\GUIMenu");
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("Fonts:", fallback + "\\Fonts");
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("Text:", fallback + "\\Text");
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("misc:", fallback + "\\misc");
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("sounds:", fallback + "\\sounds");
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("D:", fallback);
        fs->RegisterSymbolicLink("d:", fallback);
      }
      REXLOG_INFO("neverdead: VFS fallback aliases registered (dvd:, cdrom0:, assets:, FMV:, GUIMenu:, Fonts:, Text:, misc:, sounds:, D:, d:)");
    }
  }
};
